# packages/experimental/webworker-runtime

钉住提交 `cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc` 上这一组的逐文件机制普查，共 83 个文件、774 条证据行。判据、读法与全仓库口径见 [`../census-index.md`](../census-index.md)。

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/README.md

这个包的英文 README，说明浏览器 Worker 宿主的三个产物、页面半边的启动流程与已知限制。

- 无运行期机制

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/package.json

这个包的清单，声明模块类型、入口映射与发布文件集。

- `"type": "module"` 与 `main`/`types` 指定包按 ESM 解析、默认入口为 `lib/index.js`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/package.json:11-13](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/package.json#L11-L13)）
- `exports` 开出五个可解析入口：根、`./invariant`、`./src/*` 源码直读、`./worker` 指向 `lib/worker.js`、`./client` 指向 `lib/client.js`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/package.json:14-30](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/package.json#L14-L30)）
- `"private": true` 阻止该包被发布（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/package.json:5](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/package.json#L5)）
- `files` 把打包内容限定为四个 `lib/*.js` 与 `lib/types` 下的声明文件（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/package.json:69-75](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/package.json#L69-L75)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts

页面侧的注入表解释器，把 boot 载荷里的结构化 `IndexInjection` 行在页面上逐条执行；由 `client/index.ts` 的连接流程调用。

- 遇到未知行类型时抛出带 JSON 序列化内容的错误（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts:9-11](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts#L9-L11)）
- 按数组顺序串行执行每一行（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts:23](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts#L23)）
- `global` 行把 `row.value` 写到 `globalThis[row.name]`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts:25-27](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts#L25-L27)）
- `script` 行创建内联 `<script>` 并按 `placement` 追加到 head 或 body（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts:28-33](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts#L28-L33)）
- `script-src` 行 await 传入的 `loadScript` 取脚本并执行（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts:34-35](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts#L34-L35)）
- `script-preload` 行被跳过，不发起任何请求（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts:37-40](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts#L37-L40)）
- `style` 行创建 `<style>` 并追加到 head（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts:41-46](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts#L41-L46)）
- `html` 行按 `placement` 用 `insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend')` 插入片段（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts:47-49](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/apply-injections.ts#L47-L49)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts

postMessage 隧道的页面半边：把 fetch 形状的调用转成 `req` 帧，并从 worker 的响应帧重建 `Response`；被 `client/index.ts` 与页面上的传输全局使用。

- `TunnelLogicalStreamError` 把远端失败的 `code`/`details` 或载体失败标记挂在错误对象上（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:43-53](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L43-L53)）
- `LogicalStreamInbox` 缓存流帧、以 `wake` 回调唤醒等待者，`fail` 后清空队列并让后续 `next()` 抛出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:55-84](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L55-L84)）
- 状态码阈值 500 决定哪些响应会被记为拒绝（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:90](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L90)）
- `base64` 以 32 KiB 分块拼接字符，再调用 `btoa`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:96-104](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L96-L104)）
- `localizeSourceMap` 抓取 sourceMappingURL 注释、经隧道取回内容并替换为 Base64 data URL，取回失败或非 ok 时直接删掉该注释（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:106-120](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L106-L120)）
- `toBodyBuffer` 把字符串、ArrayBuffer、视图归一为可转移的 ArrayBuffer，其它类型抛错（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:122-131](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L122-L131)）
- 101/204/205/304 被列为必须携带空 body 的状态（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:133-134](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L133-L134)）
- 构造函数订阅 worker 的 `message` 事件把每帧交给 `receive`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:160-164](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L160-L164)）
- worker `error` 事件把所有在飞请求写入控制台、拒绝所有 unary、error 所有 body 流、fail 所有逻辑流并释放监听（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:165-181](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L165-L181)）
- `init` 发出携带基础镜像与有序 overlay URL 的 `init` 帧（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:189-191](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L189-L191)）
- `fetch` 在发帧前检查 signal 已 abort 则直接抛 `AbortError`，请求不到达 worker（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:194-198](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L194-L198)）
- `fetch` 自增 id、组装 method/url/headers/body 的 `req` 帧并 postMessage，同时登记 pending 与在飞描述（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:199-214](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L199-L214)）
- 带 signal 的 `fetch` 把响应与 abort 拒绝竞速，若响应为流式则把 signal 转交给 body 阶段，最后释放竞速监听（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:215-225](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L215-L225)）
- `open` 发 `stream-open` 帧、逐条 yield `stream-item`、遇 `stream-error` 抛出，finally 清理注册并对未终结的流补发 abort（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:235-273](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L235-L273)）
- `bootPayload` 请求 `/__boot__`，非 ok 时抛出带状态码与响应文本的错误（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:279-285](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L279-L285)）
- `loadBundle` 经隧道取包体、内联其 source map、以 Blob URL 作为 `<script src>` 执行并在 load/error 后移除元素与撤销 URL（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:295-319](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L295-L319)）
- `rejectOnAbort` 在 signal 上挂一次性 abort 监听并返回释放函数，避免长生命周期 signal 累积监听（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:321-335](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L321-L335)）
- `abortRequest` 清空该 id 的 unary/body/在飞/释放登记、向 worker 发 abort 帧并让 body 流控制器出错（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:343-353](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L343-L353)）
- `observeStreamAbort` 在 body 阶段重新挂 abort 监听并记录其释放函数（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:362-366](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L362-L366)）
- `cancelStream` 在读者取消 ReadableStream 时释放监听、删除登记并向 worker 发 abort（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:376-381](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L376-L381)）
- `abortWorkerOperation` 发 `abort` 帧并吞掉 postMessage 自身的失败（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:384-391](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L384-L391)）
- `warnRefusal` 在页面 console 打印请求 id、方法与 URL 以及回来的结果（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:402-404](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L402-L404)）
- `res` 帧在状态 ≥500 时先告警，然后按空 body 状态集决定 body，并 resolve 一个 `Response`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:408-421](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L408-L421)）
- `res-head` 帧建立 `ReadableStream`、登记其控制器并以流为 body resolve `Response`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:422-436](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L422-L436)）
- `res-chunk` 帧把字节 enqueue 进对应流（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:437-440](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L437-L440)）
- `res-end` 帧关闭流并清掉 body/在飞登记与 abort 监听（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:441-449](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L441-L449)）
- `res-err` 帧告警后拒绝仍在等待的 unary，或让已开始的 body 流出错（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:450-466](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L450-L466)）
- `stream-item`/`stream-end`/`stream-error` 帧被推入对应逻辑流 inbox，未知帧类型抛错（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts:467-476](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/client.ts#L467-L476)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/index.ts

页面半边的对外入口，提供可选的来源选择阶段与 Worker 连接握手；由承载页面直接 import 并传入自己静态打包的 Worker。

- `bootReadyGate` 在 `globalThis.__DSH_BOOT_READY__` 上惰性安装一个共享的 deferred（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/index.ts:75-77](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/index.ts#L75-L77)）
- `holdWorkerHostBoot` 提前装上启动栅栏并订阅其 rejection 以免变成未处理拒绝（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/index.ts:84-89](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/index.ts#L84-L89)）
- `chooseWorkerHostSource` 持住启动栅栏、把镜像与清单 URL 相对 `document.baseURI` 解析、运行选择器并在失败时 reject 栅栏后重抛（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/index.ts:99-112](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/index.ts#L99-L112)）
- `connectWorkerHost` 构造 `WorkerTunnel` 并发出携带基础镜像与有序 overlay 绝对 URL 的 `init`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/index.ts:140-144](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/index.ts#L140-L144)）
- 握手先取回 boot 载荷，再把 `fetch`/`openStream`/`loadBundle`/`ownsHost: true` 装到 `globalThis.__DSH_TRANSPORT__`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/index.ts:145-153](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/index.ts#L145-L153)）
- 传输全局装好后才执行注入表，随后 resolve 启动栅栏；任一步失败则 reject 栅栏并重抛（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/index.ts:154-160](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/index.ts#L154-L160)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts

Worker 启动前的文件系统来源选择器，读取查询参数或渲染一张卡片让用户挑选内置 fixture，返回要挂载的 overlay 列表。

- `escapeMarkup` 把五个字符转义后才拼进选项标记（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts:168-170](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts#L168-L170)）
- `optionMarkup` 生成 radio 输入并按当前选中与 disabled 状态置属性（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts:172-180](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts#L172-L180)）
- `fixtureChoices` 把清单里的 overlay 相对路径按清单 URL 解析成绝对 URL（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts:182-189](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts#L182-L189)）
- 查询参数 `preview-fixture=none` 直接返回空 overlay，完全跳过清单请求与 UI（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts:196-198](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts#L196-L198)）
- fixture 清单以 HTTP 取回，非 ok 时抛出带状态码的错误（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts:200-203](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts#L200-L203)）
- 选项集合由空环境、清单里的 fixture 与一个禁用的 WebFS 条目按此顺序组成（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts:204-220](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts#L204-L220)）
- 带有其它 `preview-fixture` 值时直接返回该项的 overlays，未知或禁用的值抛错（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts:221-227](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts#L221-L227)）
- 交互路径要求页面存在 `#root`，缺失即抛（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts:229-230](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts#L229-L230)）
- 默认选中项取自清单的 `defaultFixture`，否则为空环境（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts:231](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts#L231)）
- 向 head 追加样式、向 `#root` 前插一张表单卡片作为选择界面（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts:232-250](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts#L232-L250)）
- 一次性 `submit` 监听阻止默认提交、从 FormData 读取选中值，读不到字符串则拒绝（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts:251-258](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts#L251-L258)）
- 提交后再次校验该项存在且未禁用，然后移除卡片与样式并返回其 overlays（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts:259-263](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/client/source-chooser.ts#L259-L263)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts

打包期的模块降级变换：一次 acorn 解析把 ES 模块改写成 CommonJS 体，并把每个挂起点接到 ambient store 协议上；镜像打包器是它的唯一调用方。

- 运行期 helper 源码表定义 `def`/`default`/`ns`/`exportAll`/`dynImport` 的实际实现文本（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:20-26](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L20-L26)）
- helper 依赖表让 `exportAll` 带出 `def`、`dynImport` 带出 `ns`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:28-31](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L28-L31)）
- 挂起协议统一走名为 `__als` 的自由变量（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:34](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L34)）
- 构造时把开头的 `#!` 改写成 `//`，保持每个字符偏移与行数不变（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:75-79](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L75-L79)）
- `fail` 把偏移换算成行号并抛出带路径与行号的错误（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:81-84](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L81-L84)）
- `helper` 递归登记依赖后返回 helper 标识符（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:86-90](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L86-L90)）
- `edit` 用原区间换行数减去替换文本换行数的差补空行，使输出行号与输入一致（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:110-120](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L110-L120)）
- `literal` 要求模块说明符是字符串字面量，并把它记入静态请求集（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:134-139](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L134-L139)）
- `requests()`/`metaRequests()` 按首次出现顺序导出两类静态请求（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:141-149](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L141-L149)）
- import 声明带 import attributes 时直接失败（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:153-157](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L153-L157)）
- 无 specifier 的 import 被改成裸 `require(...)` 语句（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:158-164](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L158-L164)）
- 默认、命名空间与具名 import 分别改写为 `__dsh$default`、`__dsh$ns` 与属性读取（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:165-181](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L165-L181)）
- `export const/function/class` 只删关键字并把声明引入的绑定登记到 exports 表（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:190-197](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L190-L197)）
- 带 `from` 的具名再导出改写为 `require` 加逐个 getter 定义（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:198-209](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L198-L209)）
- 裸 `export { a as b }` 登记绑定并整段删除（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:210-214](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L210-L214)）
- `export default` 的关键字段被替换为 `exports.default = `（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:217-221](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L217-L221)）
- `export *` 改写为 `__dsh$exportAll`，具名形式改写为 ns 取值加一个 getter（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:223-238](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L223-L238)）
- 每个 `await` 被包成 `__als.resume(await __als.pause(...))`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:242-247](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L242-L247)）
- `for await` 被展开成显式迭代器循环，每次 next 走 pause/resume，非正常结束时在 finally 里 await `close`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:254-278](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L254-L278)）
- 异步生成器里的普通 `yield` 被包成 `__als.afterYield(__als.snapshot(), …)`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:287-291](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L287-L291)）
- `yield*` 只允许作为整条表达式语句出现，缺操作数或被包在别的表达式里都失败（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:292-301](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L292-L301)）
- `yield*` 被展开成显式委托循环，把消费者送入值回传给内层迭代器并在 finally 里关闭（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:302-313](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L302-L313)）
- 动态 `import(...)` 的关键字被换成 `__dsh$dynImport`，字面量参数进入静态请求集、计算参数不进（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:337-346](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L337-L346)）
- 字面量 `require(...)` 调用（含模块作用域内的 `createRequire(import.meta.url)(...)`）记入静态请求集（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:347-357](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L347-L357)）
- 字面量 `import.meta.resolve(...)` 记入独立的 metaResolve 请求集（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:358-369](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L358-L369)）
- `import.meta` 被替换为自由变量 `__dsh$meta`，`new.target` 保持原样（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:372-381](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L372-L381)）
- 顶层 `await` 与顶层 `for await` 一律失败，带标签的 `for await` 也失败（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:382-401](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L382-L401)）
- 进入函数体时重算 asyncGenerator 标志、函数深度并关闭模块作用域（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:405-421](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L405-L421)）
- `isCreateRequireCall` 只认已登记绑定且单参数为 `import.meta.url` 的调用（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:431-451](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L431-L451)）
- `indexCreateRequireImports` 从 `node:module`/`module` 的具名 import 里收集 `createRequire` 的本地名（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:453-463](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L453-L463)）
- 源码里已含 `__als.pause(` 或 `__als$` 时判定为重复降级并失败（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:465-471](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L465-L471)）
- 以 module sourceType、允许函数外 await 的方式 acorn 解析，失败即抛（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:472-481](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L472-L481)）
- 无任何编辑且没有模块语法时原样返回源码（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:484](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L484)）
- 前言写入 `"use strict"` 与 `__esModule` 标记、被用到的 helper 源码，以及每个导出绑定的 getter（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:486-494](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L486-L494)）
- 编辑按起止排序后以区间切片方式递归渲染，嵌套区间内的编辑也照样生效（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:496-507](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L496-L507)）
- 产物以 script sourceType、不允许函数外 await 的方式重新解析，通不过即失败（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:508-514](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L508-L514)）
- `declaredBindings` 递归展开对象、数组、默认值与 rest 模式，遇到不支持的模式或匿名声明即失败（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:525-559](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L525-L559)）
- 变换结果按源码文本缓存，同一份源码只解析一次（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:567-585](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L567-L585)）
- `lowerModuleSource` 以产物是否不等于输入来判定 `lowered`，并连同两类请求一起返回给打包器（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts:622-625](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/compile/transform.ts#L622-L625)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts

内置 Preview overlay 目录的格式定义与校验器，被来源选择器在取回 `fixtures.json` 之后调用。

- 清单版本号固定为 1（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts:4](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts#L4)）
- 清单文件名固定为 `fixtures.json`，按基础镜像 URL 解析（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts:7](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts#L7)）
- 顶层不是对象、版本不符或 `fixtures` 非数组时抛错（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts:36-40](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts#L36-L40)）
- 每条 fixture 的 id 必须匹配小写短横线正则且不得为 `none`/`webfs`，label、description 非空，overlays 必须是非空且全为非空字符串的数组（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts:43-58](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts#L43-L58)）
- 重复 id 抛错（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts:59-60](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts#L59-L60)）
- `defaultFixture` 非 null 时必须命中已收集的 id，否则抛错（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts:63-66](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts#L63-L66)）
- 返回的是重建出来的独立对象，而不是解析得到的原 JSON（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts:67](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/fixture-manifest.ts#L67)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts

镜像布局的常量集合，打包器按它写、Worker 宿主按它挂载与包裹模块。

- 虚拟根目录默认为 `/dsh`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts:9](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts#L9)）
- 基础镜像文件名固定为 `vfs-image.tar.gz`，页面从页面同级路径取它（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts:16](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts#L16)）
- 组合出的 profile 固定写在镜像内 `config/cordis.yml`，Loader 从这里读（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts:19](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts#L19)）
- 降级清单固定在 `config/vfs-manifest.json`，运行时在包裹任何模块前读它（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts:22](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts#L22)）
- 根下的 `home` 目录是 process shim 的 `DSH_HOME`/`HOME` 默认值（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts:25](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts#L25)）
- 每个镜像都携带空的 `home/`、`workspace/`、`tmp/` 三个目录（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts:28](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts#L28)）
- overlay 只允许填充 `home` 与 `workspace` 两个顶层目录（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts:34](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts#L34)）
- `LOWERING_VERSION` 是打包器写入清单、宿主挂载时校验的降级代码形态标识（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts:42](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts#L42)）
- `WRAPPER_PARAMS` 按序固定降级模块体期望的七个自由变量名（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts:51-53](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/image-layout.ts#L51-L53)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/index.ts

包的根入口，把 ALS 运行时、帧解析、模块加载器、路径工具、变换、隧道、process shim、Worker 宿主、镜像布局、fixture 清单与存储层的符号统一重导出。

- 无运行期机制

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/invariant.ts

这个包的 invariant 伴生插件，向 invariants 服务登记包名。

- 插件名与所需注入服务被声明为 `webworker-runtime-invariant` 与 `invariants`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/invariant.ts:13-15](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/invariant.ts#L13-L15)）
- 安装器为空实现，不注册任何运行期检查（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/invariant.ts:23](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/invariant.ts#L23)）
- `apply` 用包名和空安装器调用 `ctx.invariants.register` 并返回其 disposer（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/invariant.ts:30-31](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/invariant.ts#L30-L31)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-proxies.ts

模块替换表，构建时转成打包器别名、运行时转成加载器的静态模块表。

- 精确说明符表把 `node:fs`、`fs/promises`、`path`、`os`、`url`、`module`、`crypto`、`buffer`、`http`、`async_hooks`、`util`、`events`、`timers/promises`、`perf_hooks`、`tty`、`zlib`、`child_process` 指向 worker 侧的实现模块（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-proxies.ts:23-58](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-proxies.ts#L23-L58)）
- `dns/promises`、`net`、`vm`、`worker_threads`、`sqlite` 指向结构性 mock，符号齐全但调用即抛（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-proxies.ts:59-66](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-proxies.ts#L59-L66)）
- `koffi`、`sharp`、`node-pty`、`@vscode/ripgrep`、`@earendil-works/pi-ai`、`ws` 被换成 worker 侧替身（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-proxies.ts:67-74](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-proxies.ts#L67-L74)）
- 前缀表让 `@earendil-works/pi-ai/` 下的任意子路径都落到同一个替身模块（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-proxies.ts:79-81](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-proxies.ts#L79-L81)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts

Worker VFS 之上的 CommonJS 加载器，填 Cordis 的 `loader.internal` 入口导入接缝，并支撑 `node:module` 的 `createRequire`。

- 条件解析顺序固定为 `browser`、`require`、`import`、`default`，不含 `node`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:19](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L19)）
- 补全探测的扩展名固定为 `.js`、`.json`、`.mjs`、`.cjs`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:22](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L22)）
- 静态模块表用 Map 承载，使命中 Object 原型成员名的说明符照常落空（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:138-140](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L138-L140)）
- 前缀替身按键长度倒序排列，使最长前缀优先命中（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:141-142](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L141-L142)）
- 构造时按可选的 `alsCausality` 建出 ALS 运行时，后续注入每个模块体（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:143-144](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L143-L144)）
- `resolveInternal` 把静态模块报成 `builtin` 并回显说明符，把文件报成 `json` 或 `commonjs` 并给出 file URL（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:145-153](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L145-L153)）
- `internal` 暴露 `import`/`resolve`/`resolveSync` 三个面，缺省父 URL 时以虚拟根为基准（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:154-162](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L154-L162)）
- 失败信息附带当前 importer 调用链（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:165-168](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L165-L168)）
- `baseDirectoryOf` 把 file URL 转路径，并按 VFS 里是目录还是文件决定用自身还是父目录（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:171-176](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L171-L176)）
- `manifestOf` 按目录缓存 package.json，JSON 非法或不是对象时失败（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:178-193](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L178-L193)）
- `selectExport` 区分子路径映射与条件映射，先精确匹配子路径，再按 `*` 通配前后缀捕获并回填（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:196-228](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L196-L228)）
- `selectCondition` 按声明顺序取第一个落在条件集内且能解出目标的分支，数组按序回退（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:231-247](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L231-L247)）
- `probe` 先试原路径与四个扩展名，再对目录读 `main` 或退到 `index`，全落空则失败并列出候选（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:250-263](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L250-L263)）
- `staticModule` 先精确命中、再最长前缀、最后按补 `node:` 前缀查一次（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:266-273](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L266-L273)）
- 静态模块优先于一切文件解析，未注册的 `cordis:`/`node:` 说明符直接失败（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:281-286](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L281-L286)）
- file URL、相对与绝对说明符各自转成路径后走 `probe`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:287-295](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L287-L295)）
- 裸说明符按 scope 切出包名，只在虚拟根的 `node_modules` 下查一层，缺 package.json 即失败（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:296-302](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L296-L302)）
- 有 `exports` 的包只走 exports，未命中子路径时报出当前条件集；无 `exports` 的包退到 `main` 或原子路径（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:303-313](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L303-L313)）
- 静态解析直接调工厂函数取模块对象（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:322-323](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L322-L323)）
- 按解析后的绝对路径缓存模块记录，命中即返回其 exports（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:324-326](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L324-L326)）
- `.json` 文件被 `JSON.parse` 后作为 exports 缓存（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:327-331](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L327-L331)）
- 执行模块体前先把记录放进缓存，使循环依赖读到部分导出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:332-335](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L332-L335)）
- 模块体被喂入 exports、目录绑定的 require、module、`__filename`、`__dirname`、含 `url`/`resolve` 的 meta 对象与 ALS 运行时（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:336-357](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L336-L357)）
- 模块体抛错时从缓存删除该记录并重抛，importer 栈在 finally 里弹出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:359-364](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L359-L364)）
- `compile` 用 `new Function` 以固定的 wrapper 参数名包裹镜像里的模块体（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:377-380](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L377-L380)）
- 语法错误按 await 与 import/export 两类分别报成顶层 await 不可用与镜像未被降级，其余报通用编译失败（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:381-390](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L381-L390)）
- `requireFrom` 造出目录绑定的 require，其 `resolve` 对 worker 提供的静态模块失败并说明它没有 VFS 路径（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:398-406](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L398-L406)）
- `resolve.paths` 对静态与 `node:` 说明符返回 null，相对说明符返回当前目录，其余返回根下 `node_modules`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:407-411](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L407-L411)）
- `createRequire` 把模块路径、目录或 file URL 折成目录后交给 `requireFrom`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:420-422](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L420-L422)）
- `usage` 报出已执行的模块体数量，供宿主的启动诊断读取（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:428-430](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L428-L430)）
- 模块级单例发布/读取当前加载器，未发布时读取会抛出说明必须先调用 setter 的错误（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts:433-452](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/module-loader.ts#L433-L452)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts

Worker VFS 的 POSIX 路径工具，被模块加载器与 VFS 用来把路径规范成统一的绝对形式。

- `normalize` 折叠空段与 `.`、按是否绝对路径决定 `..` 能否越过根、并保留结尾斜杠（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts:25-42](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts#L25-L42)）
- `join` 过滤空段拼接后规范化，全空时返回 `.`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts:49-52](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts#L49-L52)）
- `resolve` 从右往左拼接，遇到第一个绝对段即停止，结果总是绝对路径（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts:59-67](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts#L59-L67)）
- `dirname` 先规范化再取父段，根的子项返回 `/`、裸名返回 `.`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts:74-80](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts#L74-L80)）
- `basename` 先规范化再取末段，并可按后缀裁剪（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts:88-93](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts#L88-L93)）
- `extname` 对以点开头的名字返回空串（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts:100-104](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts#L100-L104)）
- `isAbsolute` 只看是否以 `/` 开头（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts:111-113](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts#L111-L113)）
- `relative` 先各自 resolve 再求公共前缀，用 `..` 段补齐差额（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts:121-128](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts#L121-L128)）
- `parse` 输出 root/dir/base/ext/name 五项，dir 与 base 同样是规范化后的（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts:135-140](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts#L135-L140)）
- `toNamespacedPath` 原样返回入参（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts:147-149](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts#L147-L149)）
- `pathToFileUrl` 先 resolve 再逐段 `encodeURIComponent` 拼成 `file://` 文本（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts:156-159](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts#L156-L159)）
- `fileUrlToPath` 对非 `file://` 输入抛错，并剥掉 query/fragment 后解码，空结果回落到 `/`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts:166-170](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/module-system/posix-path.ts#L166-L170)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/abort-error.ts

构造 Node 形状取消错误的小工具，被各个可取消的 builtin 实现共用。

- 构造出的错误固定带 `name: 'AbortError'` 与 `code: 'ABORT_ERR'`，传入 reason 时挂到 `cause` 上（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/abort-error.ts:8-14](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/abort-error.ts#L8-L14)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts

Worker 侧的 `node:async_hooks` 替身，用显式切换模型实现 `AsyncLocalStorage`，并给降级变换提供 snapshot/restore 两个面。

- 取下未被 hook 层包过的原始 `Promise.prototype.then` 供本模块自己记账（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:58](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L58)）
- 模块级 `instances` 集合登记每一个被构造出来的实例（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:61](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L61)）
- 构造函数把自身加入实例集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:75-77](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L75-L77)）
- `run` 同时压入折叠栈条目与 ambient 槽，两者都按对象身份而非位置移除（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:87-106](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L87-L106)）
- 进入 `run` 前清掉 overlay 与 resumed 槽并记住旧值，使新边界不再读到外面的上下文（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:107-113](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L107-L113)）
- 回调同步抛出时还原 overlay、结束边界再重抛（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:114-121](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L114-L121)）
- 返回值非 thenable 时边界同步结束，是 thenable 时用原始 `then` 在两种结局上都结束边界，`then` 本身抛出则就地结束（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:122-137](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L122-L137)）
- `getStore` 按 overlay、resumed、最内层 ambient、折叠栈栈顶的顺序取值（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:146-152](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L146-L152)）
- `exit` 以 `undefined` 走同一条 `run` 路径（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:160-162](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L160-L162)）
- `enterWith` 直接压入一条不会自动移除的折叠栈条目（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:169-171](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L169-L171)）
- `disable` 一次清空四个槽（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:173-179](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L173-L179)）
- `snapshotAll` 复制每个存活实例的当前有效 store，包含读到 `undefined` 的实例（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:186-188](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L186-L188)）
- `restoreAll` 把快照装进各实例的 resumed 槽，返回的 disposer 只在槽仍是自己装的那个时才还原（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:195-207](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L195-L207)）
- `captureContext` 跳过读到 `undefined` 的实例，全部为空时返回 `undefined`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:214-223](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L214-L223)）
- `runWithContext` 把快照装进 overlay 槽并在 finally 里逐个还原原值（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:231-243](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L231-L243)）
- `bind`/`snapshot` 两个静态方法把当前上下文捕获成可复用的调用包装（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:258-270](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L258-L270)）
- `bindAsyncContext` 在没有任何活动上下文时原样返回入参函数，不做包装（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:309-314](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L309-L314)）
- `runAtAsyncContextRoot` 把每个存活实例都置为 `undefined` 后再运行回调（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:323-326](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L323-L326)）
- `__snapshotAll`/`__restoreAll` 是降级代码经模块替换表直接调用的暂停与恢复点（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:337-351](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L337-L351)）
- `alsCausality` 把这对函数收成加载器消费的 `snapshot`/`restore` 面，`restore` 丢弃 disposer（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:358-361](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L358-L361)）
- `executionAsyncId` 恒返回 1、`triggerAsyncId` 恒返回 0（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:367-377](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L367-L377)）
- `createHook` 调用即抛，`AsyncResource` 走 notImplementedFail（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:383-389](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L383-L389)）
- `__esModule` 标记与默认导出决定 `require()` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:392](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L392)）
- 默认导出只暴露五个成员（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts:404-406](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/async_hooks.ts#L404-L406)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/buffer.ts

Worker 侧的 `node:buffer` 替身，转接 `buffer` npm 包并安装对应全局。

- 模块求值时就用 `defineProperty` 把 `Buffer` 装到 `globalThis` 上（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/buffer.ts:8](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/buffer.ts#L8)）
- `constants` 用 npm 包的 `kMaxLength` 与硬写的 `MAX_STRING_LENGTH` 组成（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/buffer.ts:16-19](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/buffer.ts#L16-L19)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require()` 得到的三个成员（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/buffer.ts:22-28](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/buffer.ts#L22-L28)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts

Worker 侧的 `node:crypto` 替身：随机数走 WebCrypto，同步摘要走 `@noble/hashes`。

- 摘要算法表只收 sha1、sha256、sha512 三种（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts:13-17](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts#L13-L17)）
- `toBytes` 把字符串按 UTF-8 编码、把 ArrayBuffer 包成视图（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts:21-25](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts#L21-L25)）
- `createHash` 把算法名小写并去掉一个短横线后查表，查不到即抛（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts:39-43](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts#L39-L43)）
- `update` 只累积分块，`digest` 时才拼接并一次性哈希，按有无 encoding 返回 Buffer 或字符串（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts:44-62](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts#L44-L62)）
- `randomBytes` 用 `globalThis.crypto.getRandomValues` 填充后包成 Buffer（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts:70-74](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts#L70-L74)）
- `randomUUID` 转发到仓库自有的 mint，而不是浏览器只在安全上下文提供的 `crypto.randomUUID`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts:82-84](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts#L82-L84)）
- `getRandomValues` 直接转发给 WebCrypto 并返回同一个数组（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts:91-93](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts#L91-L93)）
- `randomInt` 取一个 32 位随机数按比例缩放到 0 至 max 之间且不含 max（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts:100-103](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts#L100-L103)）
- `webcrypto` 直接暴露 `globalThis.crypto`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts:106](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts#L106)）
- 默认导出限定 `require()` 拿到的六个成员（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts:124-126](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/crypto.ts#L124-L126)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts

Worker 的进程层：把 `spawn` 实现成在另一个 Web Worker 里跑 shell 进程，并按 `ChildProcess` 的面向子进程服务汇报。

- `WorkerReadable` 的 `setEncoding`/`pause`/`resume` 是空操作，只为让配置调用不抛（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:61-77](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L61-L77)）
- `push` 把文本转成 UTF-8 Buffer 发 `data`，已 destroy 或空串时不发（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:83-86](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L83-L86)）
- `finish` 发 `end`、`destroy` 停止投递并发 `close`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:89-98](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L89-L98)）
- `WorkerWritable` 把所有写入累积成一个字符串，`end` 时发 `finish`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:110-127](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L110-L127)）
- 子进程对象按三个 stdio 位是否为 `pipe` 决定 stdin/stdout/stderr 是流对象还是 null（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:147-153](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L147-L153)）
- `kill` 把信号交给进程表投递，默认信号为 SIGTERM（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:160-162](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L160-L162)）
- `stdioOf` 把字符串、缺省与数组三种写法归一成三元组，缺省全为 `pipe`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:166-170](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L166-L170)）
- `environmentOf` 缺省继承 `globalThis.process.env` 并过滤掉值为 undefined 的键（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:173-177](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L173-L177)）
- `spawnEnoent` 造出带 `code`/`errno`/`path`/`syscall` 的 ENOENT 错误（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:183-190](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L183-L190)）
- `shellScriptOf` 只把 `bash -c` / `sh -c` 识别成需要解释的脚本（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:193-197](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L193-L197)）
- 程序名非字符串或为空时抛 `ERR_INVALID_ARG_TYPE` 而不启动任何东西（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:215-221](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L215-L221)）
- `spawn` 先向进程表登记拿到 pid，再据此构造句柄（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:222-225](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L222-L225)）
- 输出路由：有管道就推给管道，`inherit` 打到 worker 的 console，`ignore` 丢弃（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:227-239](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L227-L239)）
- `settle` 只生效一次：释放 pid、被信号结束时把 exitCode 置 null 并带上信号名，然后结束两条输出流并依次发 `exit` 与 `close`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:241-255](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L241-L255)）
- `failSpawn` 同样只生效一次，释放 pid 后发 `error` 事件（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:256-261](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L256-L261)）
- 实际启动被推迟到一个微任务，使调用方能先挂监听并写标准输入（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:266-267](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L266-L267)）
- 工作目录缺省为 `DSH_ROOT`；命中虚拟可执行包装时先 `prepare`，其返回 `exit` 则直接输出并结算，否则改写 argv 并可能换掉文件系统（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:268-284](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L268-L284)）
- 既不是 shell 脚本也不在标准命令表里时，按 `missingExecutable` 输出结算或发 ENOENT（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:286-298](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L286-L298)）
- `startProcess` 带着脚本、argv、cwd、环境、已写入的 stdin 与输出/退出回调启动进程（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:299-308](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L299-L308)）
- 启动期间到达的信号在进程建好后补投，SIGKILL 走 destroy、其余走 interrupt（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:311-314](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L311-L314)）
- 启动过程中的任何异步异常都转成 `error` 事件而不是未处理拒绝（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:315-317](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L315-L317)）
- `spawnSync` 不抛异常而是返回 Node 形状结果：虚拟可执行的同步分支可直接给出输出与退出码，其余按已知命令或 ENOENT 填 `error`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:346-363](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L346-L363)）
- `exec` 把命令行包成 `bash -c` 走同一条路径（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:384-391](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L384-L391)）
- `execFile` 把 Node 允许的多种参数排列归一成 argv、options 与回调（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:401-411](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L401-L411)）
- `execute` 强制 `stdio: 'pipe'`、累积两条流，`close` 时非零退出码转成 `Command failed` 错误交给回调（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:414-426](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L414-L426)）
- `execSync`、`execFileSync`、`fork` 走 notImplementedFail，调用即失败（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:429-435](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L429-L435)）
- 默认导出限定 `require()` 拿到的七个成员（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts:452](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/child_process.ts#L452)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts

Worker 侧的 `node:events` 替身，提供 harness 代码实际用到的 EventEmitter 子集。

- `on` 按事件名追加监听器（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts:26-31](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts#L26-L31)）
- `once` 注册一个先自摘再调用原函数的包装器，并把原函数挂在包装器的 `listener` 上（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts:39-46](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts#L39-L46)）
- `prependListener` 把监听器插到列表头部（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts:54-59](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts#L54-L59)）
- `off` 从后往前找，按函数身份或 once 包装器的 `listener` 匹配，只删一个（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts:68-81](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts#L68-L81)）
- `removeAllListeners` 无参时清空整张表，带参时只删该事件（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts:98-102](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts#L98-L102)）
- `emit` 先复制监听器列表再逐个同步调用，并返回是否有监听器（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts:110-115](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts#L110-L115)）
- `listeners` 返回列表副本、`listenerCount` 返回长度（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts:122-133](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts#L122-L133)）
- `setMaxListeners` 是空操作，只返回自身（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts:139-141](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts#L139-L141)）
- 默认导出只暴露 `EventEmitter`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts:156](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/events.ts#L156)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts

Worker 内 `node:fs` 的文件监视实现，基于活动 VFS 的变更订阅，被同目录 `fs.ts` 再导出为 `watch` / `watchFile` / `unwatchFile` / `promises.watch`。

- `asPath` 把字符串、`URL`、字节序列统一 `resolve` 成绝对路径（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:29-33](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L29-L33)）
- `missingStats` 构造路径不存在时对外报出的全零 stats，`bigint` 选择数值或 BigInt 字段集（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:35-62](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L35-L62)）
- `statOrMissing` 把 `ENOENT` 转成 `missingStats`，其他错误继续抛出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:64-71](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L64-L71)）
- `statsChanged` 按 size/mtimeMs/mode/ino 与 isFile/isDirectory 判定两次 stat 是否变化（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:73-79](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L73-L79)）
- `contains` / `overlaps` 用前缀加分隔符判定路径包含关系，根路径包含一切（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:81-84](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L81-L84)）
- `FSWatcher` 构造时捕获异步上下文、订阅 VFS 变更，命中后在微任务中于该上下文里 emit `change`，事件类型按 `write` 且未改变条目或 `chmod` 判为 `change`、其余判为 `rename`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:102-114](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L102-L114)）
