- 构造时若 `signal` 已 abort 则立即 `close` 并返回，否则注册一次性 abort 监听触发 `close`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:115-121](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L115-L121)）
- `matches` 决定哪些变更被投递：同路径命中、`remove` 且删除的是目标祖先命中、目录监视按 `recursive` 决定是否只收直接子项（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:124-131](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L124-L131)）
- `filename` 决定事件里的文件名：目录监视递归时给相对路径、非递归时给首段，否则给目标 basename，`encoding: 'buffer'` 时转 Buffer（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:133-139](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L133-L139)）
- `close` 幂等地退订 VFS、摘掉 abort 监听，并在微任务中 emit 一次 `close`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:142-148](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L142-L148)）
- `ref` / `unref` / `hasRef` 只读写一个布尔标志，不影响事件循环（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:154-174](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L154-L174)）
- `watch` 归一化「选项 / 编码字符串 / 监听器」三种第二参数形态，先 `statSync` 目标（不存在即抛）再据其是否目录构造 `FSWatcher`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:184-196](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L184-L196)）
- `StatWatcher` 构造时取初始 stat、默认轮询间隔 5007ms、订阅 VFS 变更以触发排程，初始不存在时立即排一次（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:209-220](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L209-L220)）
- `schedule` 单飞地起一个定时器，到期重新 stat，只在初始缺失或 `statsChanged` 时于捕获的异步上下文里 emit `change`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:222-235](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L222-L235)）
- `stop` 退订、清定时器并 emit `stop`，`close` 是它的别名（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:238-250](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L238-L250)）
- `timerRef` / `timerUnref` 对定时器句柄可选调用 `ref`/`unref`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:285-292](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L285-L292)）
- `watchFile` 按路径共享一个 `StatWatcher`，首次创建时登记进表并在 `stop` 时移除，然后挂上监听器（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:303-320](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L303-L320)）
- `unwatchFile` 摘掉一个或全部监听器，监听器归零后停掉该路径的轮询（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:327-334](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L327-L334)）
- `watchAsync` 用队列加等待者把回调式 watcher 转成异步迭代器，事件先到就入队、消费者先到就直接兑现（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:373-391](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L373-L391)）
- `settleFailure` 在 abort 或 watcher 报错时清空队列、关闭 watcher，把错误给第一个等待者、其余等待者以 `done` 结束（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:361-372](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L361-L372)）
- 迭代器的 `next` 首次调用才启动 watcher，`return` / `throw` 关闭 watcher 并结束所有等待者（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts:401-427](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs-watch.ts#L401-L427)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts

Worker 内 `node:fs` 的完整实现，把 Node 的文件 API 架在内存 VFS 上，由 `../../builtins.ts` 注册为 `fs` / `node:fs` 的解析结果。

- `vfs()` 每次调用取当前活动 VFS，模块本身不持有实例（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:19](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L19)）
- `asPath` 接受字符串、`URL`、字节序列三种路径参数（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:25-29](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L25-L29)）
- `asBuffer` 用 `Buffer.from(buffer, byteOffset, byteLength)` 共享 VFS 底层字节而不复制（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:42-43](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L42-L43)）
- `Dirent` 只按「是否普通文件」回答 `isFile`/`isDirectory`，`isSymbolicLink` 恒为 false（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:46-88](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L46-L88)）
- `constants` 给出访问模式与打开标志的数值表（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:91-103](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L91-L103)）
- `readFileSync` 无编码返回共享 Buffer，`utf8`/`utf-8` 走 `TextDecoder`，其他编码经 Buffer 转字符串（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:111-117](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L111-L117)）
- `writeFileSync` / `appendFileSync` / `existsSync` 直接转发到 VFS（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:125-145](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L125-L145)）
- `statSync` 把 `bigint` 选项透传给 VFS，决定返回数值还是 BigInt 形态的 stats（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:153-155](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L153-L155)）
- `stat` 的回调形态在微任务中执行，异常走 error-first 回调而不是抛出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:163-181](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L163-L181)）
- `chmodSync` 把八进制字符串形式的 mode 解析成数值后写入 VFS（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:188-190](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L188-L190)）
- `lstatSync` / `lstat` 直接委派给 `stat` 系（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:198-214](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L198-L214)）
- `realpathSync` 交给 VFS 做纯归一化（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:221-223](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L221-L223)）
- `readdirSync` 在 `withFileTypes` 为真时对每个条目再 `statSync` 一次构造 `Dirent`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:231-239](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L231-L239)）
- `mkdtempSync` 用 `crypto.getRandomValues` 取三字节拼六位十六进制后缀并递归建目录（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:256-262](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L256-L262)）
- `rmSync` / `unlinkSync` / `renameSync` 转发到 VFS 的删除与改名（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:269-288](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L269-L288)）
- `accessSync` 只做存在性检查：调用 `realpathSync`，不检查权限位（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:294-296](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L294-L296)）
- 模块级 `openFiles` 表与从 3 起递增的 `nextFd` 维护文件描述符与读写游标（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:303-320](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L303-L320)）
- `badFileDescriptor` / `fileOf` 对未知 fd 抛出带 `code: 'EBADF'` 与 `syscall` 的错误（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:322-333](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L322-L333)）
- `readSync` 从给定位置或游标读取，位置为 null 时按实际读到的字节数推进游标（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:344-357](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L344-L357)）
- `writeSync` 对追加模式的 fd 每次写在当前文件尾，否则写在游标处并推进游标（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:365-372](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L365-L372)）
- `closeSync` 删除表项，删除失败则按 EBADF 抛出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:378-380](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L378-L380)）
- `linkSync` 让两个路径名指向同一文件标识（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:387-389](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L387-L389)）
- `openHandleSync` 对目录以 `fd = -1` 打开并把读写改写为整文件路径操作，非目录才真正占用描述符（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:417-448](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L417-L448)）
- 句柄的 `sync` / `datasync` 都调用 `vfs().flush()`，`close` 幂等释放描述符（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:449-456](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L449-L456)）
- `destroyFileStream` 在销毁时摘 abort 监听并关闭描述符，两个方向共用（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:492-504](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L492-L504)）
- `closeFileStream` 把可选完成回调挂到 `close` 事件上再 `destroy`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:512-518](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L512-L518)）
- `ReadStream` 构造时把 `autoClose` 映射为 `autoDestroy`、默认 highWaterMark 64KiB、注册一次性 abort 监听（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:537-552](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L537-L552)）
- `ReadStream._construct` 校验 start/end 区间与已 abort 的 signal，打开成功后置 fd 并 emit `open` 与 `ready`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:554-575](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L554-L575)）
- `ReadStream._read` 按 `end` 截断本次读取长度，读到 0 字节即 `push(null)` 结束，累加 `bytesRead`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:577-599](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L577-L599)）
- `WriteStream` 构造时设 `decodeStrings`、默认编码与 64KiB 缓冲，并注册 abort 监听（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:630-645](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L630-L645)）
- `WriteStream._construct` 打开后按 `start` 设定描述符起始游标，再 emit `open` 与 `ready`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:647-669](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L647-L669)）
- `WriteStream._write` 按 chunk 编码转 Buffer 后写入并累加 `bytesWritten`，fd 为空时按 EBADF 失败（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:671-685](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L671-L685)）
- `createReadStream` / `createWriteStream` 接受编码字符串或选项对象两种第二参数（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:706-718](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L706-L718)）
- `opendirSync` 一次性取完目录清单再按索引逐个吐出，`close`/`closeSync` 把索引推到末尾（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:736-750](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L736-L750)）
- `promises.writeFile` 在 flag 含 `x` 且目标已存在时抛 `EEXIST`，flag 以 `a` 开头时改走追加（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:759-773](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L759-L773)）
- `promises.cp` 目录递归复制、文件复制前先递归建父目录（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:789-799](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L789-L799)）
- `promises.watch` 绑定到 `fs-watch.ts` 的异步迭代器实现（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:807](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L807)）
- `__esModule = true` 让 worker 加载器把 `default` 交给默认导入（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:812](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L812)）
- 默认导出枚举 `require('node:fs')` 能拿到的全部成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts:839-846](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs.ts#L839-L846)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs/promises.ts

`node:fs/promises` 的模块面，把 `../fs.ts` 上的 `promises` 拆成具名导出，由 `../../builtins.ts` 注册为该说明符的解析结果。

- 解构出的具名列表决定 `import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises'` 能解析到哪些名字（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs/promises.ts:10-15](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs/promises.ts#L10-L15)）
- `__esModule = true` 与默认导出 `promises` 决定 `require` 的默认导入拿到什么（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs/promises.ts:18-20](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/fs/promises.ts#L18-L20)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts

Worker 内 `node:http` 的实现：`createServer` 返回不绑定套接字的假 Server 并截留请求监听器，供隧道把合成请求喂进真实路由表。

- 模块级 `captured` 与 `waiting` 保存已捕获的请求监听器和等待者集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts:19-20](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts#L19-L20)）
- `requestListener()` 同步返回已捕获的监听器或 undefined（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts:26-28](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts#L26-L28)）
- `whenRequestListener()` 已捕获时立即兑现，否则把 resolve 挂进等待集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts:34-37](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts#L34-L37)）
- `FakeServer` 的 `on`/`once`/`off` 只登记与摘除监听器，从不触发（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts:49-75](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts#L49-L75)）
- `listen` 不绑定任何端口，只在微任务中调用末位回调（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts:83-87](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts#L83-L87)）
- `address()` 返回固定的 `127.0.0.1` 与常量 `VIRTUAL_PORT = 3080`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts:93-95](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts#L93-L95)）
- `close` 在微任务中回调，`closeAllConnections` / `closeIdleConnections` 什么都不做（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts:102-115](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts#L102-L115)）
- `ServerResponse` 是空类，作为中间件特性探测读取的构造器标记（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts:122](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts#L122)）
- `createServer` 记住传入的监听器、逐个兑现等待者并清空等待集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts:129-136](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts#L129-L136)）
- `request` 与 `get` 抛错并在消息里指明改用 `fetch`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts:142-152](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts#L142-L152)）
- `STATUS_CODES` 只列出手写状态行会用到的十二个状态文本（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts:155-168](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts#L155-L168)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('node:http')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts:173-185](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/http.ts#L173-L185)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/module.ts

Worker 内 `node:module` 的实现，`createRequire` 交出 worker 模块加载器的同步 require。

- `createRequire` 从活动模块加载器造一个绑定到给定基路径的同步 require（含 `resolve` 与 `resolve.paths`）（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/module.ts:17-19](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/module.ts#L17-L19)）
- `builtinModules` 列出对外声称的内置模块名单（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/module.ts:22-25](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/module.ts#L22-L25)）
- `isBuiltin` 剥掉 `node:` 前缀后按该名单判定（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/module.ts:32-34](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/module.ts#L32-L34)）
- `stripTypeScriptTypes` 与 `register` 抛错并指明该成员在 worker 宿主中不可用（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/module.ts:40-50](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/module.ts#L40-L50)）
- `syncBuiltinESMExports` 是空实现（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/module.ts:53-55](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/module.ts#L53-L55)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('node:module')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/module.ts:61-74](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/module.ts#L61-L74)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts

Worker 内 `node:os` 的实现，每个值要么指向 VFS 路径、要么是固定的平台身份。

- `EOL` 固定为 `\n`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts:10](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts#L10)）
- `tmpdir()` / `homedir()` 返回 VFS 内的 `DSH_TMP` 与 `DSH_HOME`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts:17-27](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts#L17-L27)）
- `platform()` / `type()` / `arch()` 固定回答 `linux` / `Linux` / `x64`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts:33-51](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts#L33-L51)）
- `release()` / `hostname()` 返回合成的 `0.0.0-dsh-worker` 与 `dsh-worker`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts:57-67](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts#L57-L67)）
- `availableParallelism()` 取 `navigator.hardwareConcurrency` 并下限为 1（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts:73-75](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts#L73-L75)）
- `cpus()` 与 `networkInterfaces()` 返回空集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts:81-92](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts#L81-L92)）
- `constants.signals` 给出信号名到编号的映射，`errno` 与 `priority` 为空（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts:95-102](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts#L95-L102)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('node:os')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts:105-118](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/os.ts#L105-L118)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts

Worker 内 `node:path`，逐条移植 Node 的 POSIX 算法，`win32` 成员一律抛错。

- `cwd()` 优先读 `globalThis.process.cwd()`，缺失时回落到 `DSH_ROOT`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:26-29](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L26-L29)）
- `assertPath` 对非字符串路径抛 `TypeError` 并把收到的值 JSON 化进消息（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:31-35](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L31-L35)）
- `normalizeString` 按字符码逐位消解 `.` 与 `..`，`allowAboveRoot` 决定相对路径是否保留前导 `..`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:38-93](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L38-L93)）
- `resolve` 自右向左拼接直到遇到绝对段，否则再前置 `cwd()`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:100-118](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L100-L118)）
- `normalize` 保留原有的尾部分隔符，空结果按绝对/相对分别回答 `/`、`./`、`.`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:125-137](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L125-L137)）
- `isAbsolute` 只看首字符是否为斜杠（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:144-147](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L144-L147)）
- `join` 跳过空段后拼接再 `normalize`，全空时回答 `.`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:154-163](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L154-L163)）
- `relative` 先各自 `resolve`，按共同前缀段数生成对应个数的 `..` 再接剩余段（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:171-184](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L171-L184)）
- `dirname` 纯词法取父目录、不做归一化，根下单层回答 `/`、双斜杠情形回答 `//`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:191-210](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L191-L210)）
- `basename` 在给出 suffix 时逐字符反向比对后缀并按 Node 的规则决定是否剥离（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:218-265](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L218-L265)）
- `extname` 用 `preDotState` 区分隐藏文件与真正的扩展名（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:272-304](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L272-L304)）
- `format` 以 `dir ?? root` 为目录、`base ?? name + ext` 为文件名，目录等于 root 时不再补分隔符（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:311-316](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L311-L316)）
- `parse` 去掉尾部斜杠后切出 root/dir/base/ext/name（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:323-337](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L323-L337)）
- `sep` 与 `delimiter` 固定为 `/` 与 `:`，`toNamespacedPath` 原样返回（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:340-352](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L340-L352)）
- `posix` 在模块面之上加两个自引用 getter，使 `path.posix.posix` 与 `path.posix.win32` 可解（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:360-364](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L360-L364)）
- `win32` 的每个函数成员调用即抛错并写明宿主报告平台为 linux，`sep`/`delimiter` 仍给 Windows 字面量（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:366-385](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L366-L385)）
- `__esModule` 与以 `posix` 作默认导出决定 `require('node:path')` 拿到什么（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts:388-398](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/path.ts#L388-L398)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/perf_hooks.ts

Worker 内 `node:perf_hooks` 的模块面，只交出 worker 自己的高精度时钟。

- `performance` 直接绑定 `globalThis.performance`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/perf_hooks.ts:9](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/perf_hooks.ts#L9)）
- `PerformanceObserver` 由 `notImplementedFail` 生成，调用即抛出并写明模块与成员名（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/perf_hooks.ts:12-13](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/perf_hooks.ts#L12-L13)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('node:perf_hooks')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/perf_hooks.ts:16-27](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/perf_hooks.ts#L16-L27)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/stream.ts

Worker 内 `node:stream` 的模块面，转接 readable-stream 的浏览器构建。

- 从 readable-stream 取出 Duplex/Readable/Writable 等类与 `pipeline`、`finished`、`compose` 等静态成员（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/stream.ts:27-34](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/stream.ts#L27-L34)）
- 模块求值时若默认 highWaterMark 不是 64KiB 就调 `setDefaultHighWaterMark` 改成 64KiB（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/stream.ts:37-39](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/stream.ts#L37-L39)）
- `_isArrayBufferView` 用 `ArrayBuffer.isView` 实现 Node 的同名内部谓词（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/stream.ts:46](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/stream.ts#L46)）
- `streamDefault` 用 `Object.assign` 把静态帮助函数挂到 Stream 基类上作为默认导入的命名空间（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/stream.ts:49-55](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/stream.ts#L49-L55)）
- 具名导出列表、`__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `node:stream` 对外可见的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/stream.ts:57-84](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/stream.ts#L57-L84)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/timers/promises.ts

Worker 内 `node:timers/promises` 的实现，架在 worker 的定时器全局之上。

- `abortError()` 构造 `AbortError` 名字的 `DOMException` 作为中止时的拒绝原因（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/timers/promises.ts:7](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/timers/promises.ts#L7)）
- `setTimeout` 在 signal 已中止时立即拒绝且不装定时器（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/timers/promises.ts:24-27](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/timers/promises.ts#L24-L27)）
- 否则装上定时器并注册一次性 abort 监听，中止时清掉定时器并拒绝（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/timers/promises.ts:28-32](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/timers/promises.ts#L28-L32)）
- `setImmediate` 转成零延迟的 `setTimeout`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/timers/promises.ts:41-43](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/timers/promises.ts#L41-L43)）
- `scheduler.wait` / `scheduler.yield` 分别转成带延迟与零延迟的等待（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/timers/promises.ts:46-51](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/timers/promises.ts#L46-L51)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('node:timers/promises')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/timers/promises.ts:54-60](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/timers/promises.ts#L54-L60)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/tty.ts

Worker 内 `node:tty` 的模块面。

- `isatty` 恒返回 false（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/tty.ts:11-13](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/tty.ts#L11-L13)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('node:tty')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/tty.ts:16-19](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/tty.ts#L16-L19)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/url.ts

Worker 内 `node:url` 的模块面：两个路径/URL 转换加上浏览器已有的 WHATWG 类。

- `fileURLToPath` 校验协议必须是 `file:`，否则抛 `TypeError`，通过后对 pathname 做 `decodeURIComponent`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/url.ts:12-18](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/url.ts#L12-L18)）
- `pathToFileURL` 只转义 `%`、反斜杠与三个空白字符，并在非绝对路径前补斜杠（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/url.ts:25-38](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/url.ts#L25-L38)）
- `resolve` 用 `new URL(specifier, base)` 拼绝对 URL（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/url.ts:46-48](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/url.ts#L46-L48)）
- `URL` 与 `URLSearchParams` 直接转出浏览器全局类（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/url.ts:51-56](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/url.ts#L51-L56)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('node:url')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/url.ts:59-73](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/url.ts#L59-L73)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts

Worker 内 `node:util` 的实现，只提供宿主树实际导入的成员。

- `promisify` 按 error-first 约定转换，非 Error 的错误值经 `inspect` 包成 Error 再拒绝（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts:13-22](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts#L13-L22)）
- `callbackify` 取末位参数作回调，成功回传 `(null, value)`、失败回传 `(error)`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts:29-37](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts#L29-L37)）
- `inspect` 字符串加单引号、Error 取 stack、其余走 JSON 序列化并把 bigint 转字符串，序列化失败或返回 undefined 时回落到 `String(value)`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts:44-57](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts#L44-L57)）
- `format` 按 `%s`/`%d`/`%i`/`%f`/`%j`/`%o`/`%O`/`%%` 逐个替换，参数用尽后占位符原样保留，多余参数以空格追加（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts:65-79](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts#L65-L79)）
- `isDeepStrictEqual` 递归比较数组性、键数量与每个键的值（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts:87-101](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts#L87-L101)）
- `types` 提供 isPromise（含 thenable）、isDate、isRegExp、isTypedArray（排除 DataView）四个谓词（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts:104-111](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts#L104-L111)）
- `parseArgs` 调用即抛出，`deprecate` 原样返回被包装的函数（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts:117-128](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts#L117-L128)）
- `TextDecoder` / `TextEncoder` 转出浏览器全局类（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts:131-136](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts#L131-L136)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('node:util')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts:139-156](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util.ts#L139-L156)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util/types.ts

`node:util/types` 的模块面，从 `../util.ts` 的 `types` 再导出同一份谓词实现。

- 解构出的四个谓词名决定该说明符对外可解析的具名导出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util/types.ts:9](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util/types.ts#L9)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出 `types` 决定 `require` 的默认导入拿到什么（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util/types.ts:12-14](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/util/types.ts#L12-L14)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/zlib.ts

Worker 内 `node:zlib` 的模块面：保留 JSONL 会话后端在模块作用域读取的形状，所有编解码调用一律失败。

- `constants` 给出 Zstandard 参数与 flush 常量的数值（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/zlib.ts:18-28](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/zlib.ts#L18-L28)）
- 四个 zstd 一次性/回调成员由 `notImplementedFail` 生成，调用即抛出并写明模块与成员名（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/zlib.ts:31-41](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/zlib.ts#L31-L41)）
- `createZstdDecompress` 返回只有 `close` 的对象、故意不带 `_handle`/`_writeState`，让后端的私有形状探测落空（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/zlib.ts:49-51](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/zlib.ts#L49-L51)）
- `createZstdCompress` 与 gzip/gunzip 四件套同样是调用即抛的桩（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/zlib.ts:54-67](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/zlib.ts#L54-L67)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('node:zlib')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/zlib.ts:70-85](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/implemented/zlib.ts#L70-L85)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/dns/promises.ts

`node:dns/promises` 的桩模块，由 `../../../builtins.ts` 注册为该说明符的解析结果。

- `lookup` 由 `notImplementedFail` 生成，调用即抛出并写明模块与成员名（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/dns/promises.ts:11](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/dns/promises.ts#L11)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require` 的默认导入拿到什么（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/dns/promises.ts:14-20](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/dns/promises.ts#L14-L20)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts

Worker 内 `node:net` 的桩模块：只有地址判定可用，Socket 可构造但方法抛错。

- 两个正则决定 IPv4 与 IPv6 字面量的识别范围（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts:7-8](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts#L7-L8)）
- `Socket` 可构造，`write` 与 `end` 调用即抛出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts:16-26](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts#L16-L26)）
- `Socket.destroy` 是空实现，让拆解路径静默通过（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts:29-31](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts#L29-L31)）
- `isIPv4` 在正则之外还要求每段数值不超过 255（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts:39-41](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts#L39-L41)）
- `isIPv6` 要求含冒号且全为十六进制与冒号字符（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts:48-50](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts#L48-L50)）
- `isIP` 按上面两个谓词回答 4、6 或 0（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts:57-61](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts#L57-L61)）
- `createServer` 与 `connect` 调用即抛出并写明不可用（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts:67-77](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts#L67-L77)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('node:net')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts:80-90](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/net.ts#L80-L90)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/sqlite.ts

`node:sqlite` 的桩模块，由 `../../builtins.ts` 注册为该说明符的解析结果。

- `DatabaseSync` 与 `StatementSync` 由 `notImplementedFail` 生成，调用或 `new` 即抛出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/sqlite.ts:11-14](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/sqlite.ts#L11-L14)）
- `backup` 抛出 `notAvailableError` 构造的错误（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/sqlite.ts:20-22](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/sqlite.ts#L20-L22)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('node:sqlite')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/sqlite.ts:24-31](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/sqlite.ts#L24-L31)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/vm.ts

`node:vm` 的桩模块，由 `../../builtins.ts` 注册为该说明符的解析结果。

- 六个成员全部由 `notImplementedFail` 生成，调用或 `new` 即抛出并写明模块与成员名（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/vm.ts:11-26](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/vm.ts#L11-L26)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('node:vm')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/vm.ts:28-37](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/vm.ts#L28-L37)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/worker_threads.ts

`node:worker_threads` 的桩模块，线程身份值为真实值、构造类成员一律抛错。

- `Worker` 由 `notImplementedFail` 生成，构造即抛出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/worker_threads.ts:12](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/worker_threads.ts#L12)）
- `isMainThread` 为 true、`threadId` 为 0、`parentPort` 为 null、`workerData` 为 undefined（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/worker_threads.ts:15-24](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/worker_threads.ts#L15-L24)）
- `MessageChannel`、`MessagePort`、`markAsUntransferable`、`receiveMessageOnPort` 调用即抛出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/worker_threads.ts:27-38](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/worker_threads.ts#L27-L38)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('node:worker_threads')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/worker_threads.ts:41-50](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtin_modules/mock/worker_threads.ts#L41-L50)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtins.ts

Node 兼容表的唯一出处，同时供 worker 的 vite 构建做别名与 worker 模块加载器解析 `require`。

- `BUILTINS` 把每个内置说明符映射到一个返回静态模块命名空间的工厂，决定 `require('node:X')` 解析到 implemented 还是 mock 实现（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtins.ts:58-84](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtins.ts#L58-L84)）
- `path/posix` 与 `path` 指向同一个模块工厂（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtins.ts:71-72](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtins.ts#L71-L72)）
- `EXTERNALS` 把六个第三方包名整体替换成 bundle 内的替身模块（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtins.ts:87-94](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtins.ts#L87-L94)）
- `REPLACED_PREFIXES` 让加载器在精确命中落空后按最长前缀把子路径解析到同一个替身（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtins.ts:101-103](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtins.ts#L101-L103)）
- 模块求值时比对声明清单与实际接线的外部包名单，不一致就抛错，使偏差在 worker 启动而非首次 require 时暴露（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtins.ts:107-111](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtins.ts#L107-L111)）
- `createNodeBuiltins` 把外部包与内置模块合成一张表，并为每个内置名再登记一个 `node:` 前缀别名（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtins.ts:117-124](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/builtins.ts#L117-L124)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts

`koffi` 的替身模块：类型构造返回带 x64 尺寸的不透明令牌，真正跨入原生代码的入口一律抛错。

- `PRIMITIVES` 给出各原始类型在 x64 ABI 下的字节尺寸表（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts:22-49](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts#L22-L49)）
- `token` 按 `min(size, 8)` 推导对齐（尺寸为 0 时取 1）（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts:51-52](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts#L51-L52)）
- `typeOf` 对未知的原始类型名和非本桩描述符分别抛出不同消息的错误（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts:54-65](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts#L54-L65)）
- `pointer` 一律给出 8 字节的指针描述符（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts:75-77](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts#L75-L77)）
- `struct` 按 x64 补齐规则逐字段累加偏移、取最大对齐并把总尺寸向上取整（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts:87-98](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts#L87-L98)）
- `array` 的尺寸取元素尺寸乘长度、对齐沿用元素对齐（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts:106-109](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts#L106-L109)）
- `opaque` 给出尺寸 0、对齐 1 的描述符（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts:116-118](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts#L116-L118)）
- `types` 是 Proxy，任意属性读取都按名字现算类型描述符，`in` 只对原始类型表内的名字为真（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts:121-124](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts#L121-L124)）
- `alias` / `sizeof` / `alignof` 从描述符读回尺寸与对齐（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts:133-138](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts#L133-L138)）
- `load`、`alloc`、`free`、`decode`、`encode`、`address`、`register`、`unregister`、`call` 调用即抛出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts:139-147](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts#L139-L147)）
- 具名导出、`__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('koffi')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts:150-155](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/koffi.ts#L150-L155)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/node-pty.ts

`node-pty` 的替身模块，由 `../builtins.ts` 注册为该包名的解析结果。

- `spawn` 与 `open` 由 `notImplementedFail` 生成，调用即抛出并写明模块与成员名（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/node-pty.ts:10-13](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/node-pty.ts#L10-L13)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require('node-pty')` 拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/node-pty.ts:16-19](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/node-pty.ts#L16-L19)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/pi-ai.ts

某第三方 LLM 提供方 SDK 的替身模块，覆盖其主入口与子路径，目录读取返回真实 id 列表、请求路径一律抛错。

- `createProvider`、`createModels`、`getSupportedThinkingLevels`、`isContextOverflow` 调用即抛出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/pi-ai.ts:18-27](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/pi-ai.ts#L18-L27)）
- `BUILTIN_PROVIDER_IDS` 固定列出目录里的提供方 id 及其顺序（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/pi-ai.ts:30-39](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/pi-ai.ts#L30-L39)）
- `builtinProviders()` 为每个 id 造一条带 `api-key` 认证标记且模型列表为空的记录（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/pi-ai.ts:48-55](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/pi-ai.ts#L48-L55)）
- `getBuiltinProviders()` 返回 id 列表副本，`getBuiltinModels()` 返回空数组（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/pi-ai.ts:63-73](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/pi-ai.ts#L63-L73)）
- 三个 API 绑定成员调用即抛出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/pi-ai.ts:76-82](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/pi-ai.ts#L76-L82)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require` 该包时拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/pi-ai.ts:85-92](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/pi-ai.ts#L85-L92)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/replaced-externals.ts

被整体替换的第三方包名清单，无任何 import，同时被运行期内置表和构建期 VFS 镜像收集器读取。

- `REPLACED_EXTERNAL_PACKAGES` 列出六个包名，决定哪些包由 bundle 供给并被排除出 VFS 镜像（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/replaced-externals.ts:10-17](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/replaced-externals.ts#L10-L17)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ripgrep.ts

某搜索二进制包的替身模块，只交出一个路径字符串。

- `rgPath` 是固定的 `/dsh/bin/rg` 字符串，使模块作用域读取它的插件能完成构造（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ripgrep.ts:9](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ripgrep.ts#L9)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require` 该包时拿到的成员集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ripgrep.ts:12-15](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ripgrep.ts#L12-L15)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/sharp.ts

某原生图像处理包的替身模块，由 `../builtins.ts` 注册为该包名的解析结果。

- 默认导出由 `notImplementedFail` 生成，调用即抛出并写明模块与成员名（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/sharp.ts:8](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/sharp.ts#L8)）
- `__esModule` 与默认导出决定 `require` 该包时拿到什么（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/sharp.ts:11-13](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/sharp.ts#L11-L13)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ws.ts

`ws` 的替身模块：服务端类必须可构造，客户端类与需要真实升级的方法抛错。

- `WebSocket` 类的构造函数直接抛错，四个 readyState 常量仍为真实数值（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ws.ts:16-29](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ws.ts#L16-L29)）
- `WebSocketServer` 可构造，`clients` 恒为空集合（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ws.ts:32-34](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ws.ts#L32-L34)）
- `handleUpgrade` 与 `emit` 由 `notImplementedFail` 生成，调用即抛出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ws.ts:37-40](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ws.ts#L37-L40)）
- `on` 只返回自身不登记任何东西，`close` 立即同步调用回调（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ws.ts:46-56](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ws.ts#L46-L56)）
- `Server` 别名与 `__esModule` 决定 `require('ws')` 能解析到的名字（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ws.ts:13-62](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/external_packages/ws.ts#L13-L62)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/crypto.ts

补齐不安全上下文下缺失的 `crypto.randomUUID`，由 worker 启动时调用。

- `installCryptoGlobals` 在平台已提供 `randomUUID` 时直接返回，否则用可配置可写的属性描述符把自带实现装到 `globalThis.crypto` 上（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/crypto.ts:11-19](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/crypto.ts#L11-L19)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts

在任何 VFS 模块运行之前安装 `process` 全局，供 Loader 构造、`cordis.yml` 的 `!!js process.*` 表达式与第三方包的环境探测读取。

- `installProcessGlobal` 在调用时记下 `performance.now()` 作为进程起点（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts:83](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts#L83)）
- `stdout.write` / `stderr.write` 剥掉尾部换行后写 `console.log` / `console.error` 并恒返回 true（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts:84-87](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts#L84-L87)）
- `env` 复制传入环境、`argv` 复制传入向量并在缺省时用 `['node', 'dsh-webworker']`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts:89-90](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts#L89-L90)）
- `title`、`platform`、`arch`、`pid`、`version` 固定为 `dsh-webworker` / `linux` / `x64` / 1 / `v0.0.0`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts:92-97](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts#L92-L97)）
- `versions.node` 设为 `0.0.0`，使 Loader 的内部模块探测返回 undefined 而不去够 Node 内部（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts:97](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts#L97)）
- `cwd()` 返回传入的虚拟根（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts:98](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts#L98)）
- `getBuiltinModule` 走活动模块加载器解析，只有解析结果是静态模块时才求值工厂，其余情形与解析异常一律返回 undefined（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts:99-109](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts#L99-L109)）
- `kill` 对信号 0 做存活探测，不存在时抛带 `code: 'ESRCH'` 与 `syscall: 'kill'` 的错误，其余信号交给进程表投递（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts:110-119](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts#L110-L119)）
- `nextTick` 用 `queueMicrotask` 排程回调（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts:120](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts#L120)）
- 全部事件方法都是空实现：注册类返回自身、`listeners` 返回空数组、`listenerCount` 返回 0、`emit` 返回 false（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts:123-133](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts#L123-L133)）
- `hrtime.bigint()` 与 `uptime()` 都从记录的起点用 `performance.now()` 换算（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts:134-135](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts#L134-L135)）
- `exit` 不终止任何东西，只在控制台写一条警告说明 worker 继续运行（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts:136](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts#L136)）
- 构造完成后把该对象赋给 `globalThis.process` 并返回，供内置模块表填入（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts:138-139](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/process.ts#L138-L139)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/timers.ts

把 worker 的定时器全局换成 Node 形状的包装，供调用 `.unref()` 的宿主与 vendored 代码使用。

- `handleOf` 造出带 `ref`/`unref`/`hasRef` 的句柄对象，`Symbol.toPrimitive` 使其可转回数值 id（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/timers.ts:26-34](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/timers.ts#L26-L34)）
- `idOf` 让 `clear*` 同时接受数值 id 与句柄对象（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/timers.ts:36-42](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/timers.ts#L36-L42)）
- `bindHandler` 把函数型定时器回调绑定到注册时的异步上下文，字符串型回调原样透传（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/timers.ts:48-49](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/timers.ts#L48-L49)）
- `installTimerGlobals` 先绑定原生实现，再用包装覆盖 `setTimeout`/`setInterval`/`clearTimeout`/`clearInterval` 四个全局（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/timers.ts:55-64](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/timers.ts#L55-L64)）
- `setImmediate` 被装成零延迟的 `setTimeout`，`clearImmediate` 复用 `clearTimeout`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/timers.ts:65-67](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/globals/timers.ts#L65-L67)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/notImplementedFail.ts

生成结构完整但调用即失败的桩成员，被 mock 与 external_packages 下的多数替身模块使用。

- `notImplementedFail` 返回一个具名函数表达式（而非箭头函数），使桩在 `new` 下也会抛出（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/notImplementedFail.ts:26-30](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/notImplementedFail.ts#L26-L30)）
- `notAvailableError` 拼出含模块名与成员名的消息，先 `console.error` 写出再返回待抛的 Error（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/notImplementedFail.ts:40-44](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/notImplementedFail.ts#L40-L44)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/process-table.ts

Worker 自己的进程表，`node:child_process` 替身从一侧登记条目、`process` 全局从另一侧投递信号。

- 模块级 `entries` 映射与从 1 起递增的 `lastPid` 保存全部在跑的命令（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/process-table.ts:24-27](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/process-table.ts#L24-L27)）
- `registerProcess` 在命令启动前先分配 pid 并入表，使句柄可以同步报出 pid（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/process-table.ts:34-39](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/process-table.ts#L34-L39)）
- `releaseProcess` 在命令结束后移除表项（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/process-table.ts:45-47](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/process-table.ts#L45-L47)）
- `processAlive` 对 pid 取绝对值后查表，使负数的进程组 id 也能命中（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/process-table.ts:55-57](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/process-table.ts#L55-L57)）
- `signalProcess` 只记录首个到达的信号，`SIGKILL` 调 `destroy()`、其余信号调 `interrupt()`，无此条目时返回 false（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/process-table.ts:69-76](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/node/process-table.ts#L69-L76)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/als-runtime.ts

被改写模块在每个挂起点调用的运行时，负责在 `await`、`yield` 前后搬运异步上下文快照。

- `createAlsRuntime` 在没有传入 causality 时让 snapshot/restore 变成空操作，改写后的代码仍会跳一次微任务但不搬运状态（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/als-runtime.ts:48-50](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/als-runtime.ts#L48-L50)）
- `pause` 先抓快照，再把被等待值的成功与失败都收敛成一个必然兑现的 token（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/als-runtime.ts:53-59](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/als-runtime.ts#L53-L59)）
- `resume` 先恢复快照再按 token 的 `ok` 返回值或重抛错误（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/als-runtime.ts:60-64](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/als-runtime.ts#L60-L64)）
- `afterYield` 在生成器恢复时先还原快照再把传入值原样交回（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/als-runtime.ts:65-68](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/als-runtime.ts#L65-L68)）
- `iterator` 优先取 `Symbol.asyncIterator`，退而取 `Symbol.iterator` 并包成 async-from-sync 适配器，两者皆无时抛 `TypeError`（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/als-runtime.ts:69-92](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/als-runtime.ts#L69-L92)）
- `close` 调用迭代器的 `return`，抛错时吞掉并返回 undefined（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/als-runtime.ts:93-100](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/als-runtime.ts#L93-L100)）

### packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/async-context-hooks.ts

浏览器 worker 里的异步上下文补丁层，在回调注册处捕获 store、在回调执行处恢复，由 worker 入口在宿主树启动前调用一次。

- `bindSlot` 只包装函数型处理器，非函数槽位原样返回，包装后的回调在 `runWithAsyncContext(snapshot, …)` 下执行（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/async-context-hooks.ts:32-35](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/async-context-hooks.ts#L32-L35)）
- `installed` 标志使安装只生效一次，重复调用直接返回（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/async-context-hooks.ts:41-44](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/async-context-hooks.ts#L41-L44)）
- 替换 `Promise.prototype.then`：在注册时刻捕获快照，快照为 undefined 时直接走原生 `then`，否则分别包装 fulfilled/rejected 两个槽位并仍返回原生 promise（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/async-context-hooks.ts:46-61](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/async-context-hooks.ts#L46-L61)）
- 替换 `globalThis.queueMicrotask`，把回调经 `bindAsyncContext` 绑定后再交给原生实现（[packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/async-context-hooks.ts:63-66](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/cd5ef8148158c3a752a658978873241fdf8e2bbc/packages/experimental/webworker-runtime/src/polyfill/async-context/async-context-hooks.ts#L63-L66)）
